					}
				}
				create_ship = {
					type = ship_hull_cruiser_1
					equipment_variant = "Refitted Cruiser Class"
					creator = ROOT
					name = "Protector"
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_flag = refit_to_cruiser_flag
						}
					}
					set_country_flag = refit_to_cruiser_flag
					create_equipment_variant = {
						name = "Refitted Cruiser Class"
						type = light_cruiser_1
						upgrades = {
							ship_reliability_upgrade = 0
							ship_engine_upgrade = 0
							ship_gun_upgrade = 0
							ship_anti_air_upgrade = 0
						}
						obsolete = yes
					}
				}
				create_ship = {
					type = light_cruiser_1
					equipment_variant = "Refitted Cruiser Class"
					creator = ROOT
					name = "Protector"
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}

					days = 15
				}

					has_active_mission = KOR_daya_manzhou_mission

				}
				add_days_mission_timeout = {
					mission = KOR_daya_sulian_mission
					days = 15
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			add_to_variable = {
				KOR_ManZhou_Party_Size = 15
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				KOR_SOV_Party_Size = 5

				KOR_yanan_Party_Size = 10

					min = -100

	KOR_jinricheng_xuanchuan_1 = {
		priority = 80
		allowed = {
			original_tag = KOR
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = KOR_xuanchuanlan_shengyu_tt
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = {
						flag = KOR_xuanchuan_cishu_flag
						value = 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
		}
		days_remove = 90
		days_re_enable = 60
		cost = KOR_jinricheng_xuanchuan_cost
		complete_effect = {
			add_to_variable = {
				KOR_weidalingxiu_xushishi_2 = 0.05
				tooltip = research_speed_factor_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				KOR_weidalingxiu_xushishi_3 = 0.01
				tooltip = stability_weekly_tt
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_xuanchuan_cishu_flag
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = {
					flag = KOR_xuanchuan_cishu_flag
					value = 1
				}
			}
			else = {
				modify_country_flag = {
					flag = KOR_xuanchuan_cishu_flag
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = {
					KOR_weidalingxiu_xushishi_2 = -0.05
					tooltip = research_speed_factor_tt
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					KOR_weidalingxiu_xushishi_3 = -0.01
					tooltip = stability_weekly_tt
				}
			}

				flag = KOR_xuanchuan_cishu_flag
				value = -1
			}

				}
				set_country_flag = {
					flag = KOR_xuanchuan_cishu_flag
					value = 1
				}
			}
			else = {

					flag = KOR_xuanchuan_cishu_flag
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}

			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = {
					KOR_weidalingxiu_xushishi_19 = -0.1
					tooltip = industrial_capacity_factory_tt
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					KOR_weidalingxiu_xushishi_20 = -0.1
					tooltip = industrial_capacity_dockyard_tt
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					KOR_weidalingxiu_xushishi_21 = -0.15
					tooltip = line_change_production_efficiency_factor_tt
				}
			}
			modify_country_flag = {
				flag = KOR_xuanchuan_cishu_flag
				value = -1
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				KOR_jinricheng_xuanchuan_cost = 2

					is_core_of = FROM
				}
				add_core_of = KOR
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					has_capitulated = yes
					NOT = {
						is_subject_of = KOR
					}
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_zhenghe_lingtu_flag
		}
	}
}

		}

					}

			FROM = {

				}
			}

				}
			}

					command_power > 74
				}
			}

				factor = 2

			}
		}
	}
